    69	            let attempt=(||{
    70	                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
    71	                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
    72	            })();
    73	            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
    74	        }
    75	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    76	    }
    77	    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    78	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    79	    Ok(engine)
    80	}
    81	
    82	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
    83	pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    84	    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    85	    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    86	    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    87	    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.columns.contains_key("role")||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name).is_some()&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    88	    let creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    89	    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    90	    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
    91	    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    92	    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
    93	    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
    94	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
    95	        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
    96	        for (table,row) in pending{
    97	            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
    98	            let gate=&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert;
    99	            if !actors.iter().any(|actor|matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable))) {retry.push((table,row));continue;}
   100	            let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   101	            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
   102	            if candidate.seed_at([(table.clone(),seeded)],NOW).is_ok(){engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
   103	        }
   104	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
   105	    }
   106	    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
   107	    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
   108	    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
   109	    let mut attempts=0usize;
   110	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
   111	        let before=readback(&engine)?;
   112	        for bp in &creators{
   113	            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   114	            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
   115	            let current=readback(&engine)?;
   116	            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   117	            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
   118	            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(p,&current,&actors,out);
   119	            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &actors{
   120	                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
   121	                attempts+=1;
   122	                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   123	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
   124	                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
   125	                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
   126	                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
   127	                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
   128	                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
   129	                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
   130	                }
   131	            }}
   132	        }
   133	        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
   134	    }
   135	    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
   136	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
   137	}
   138	fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
   139	    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
   140	    match row{Value::Row(fields)=>Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect()),_=>row.clone()}
   141	}
     1	//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";
     6	
     7	fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
     8	    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
     9	    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
    10	        let line = line.trim();
    11	        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    12	    }).count())
    13	}
    14	
    15	/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
    16	/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
    17	/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
    18	pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    19	    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    20	    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    21	    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    22	    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    23	    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    24	    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
    25	        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
    26	            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
    27	            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
    28	            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
    29	        });
    30	        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
    31	            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
    32	            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    33	        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
    34	            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
    35	            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    36	                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
    37	                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
    38	                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
    39	                if cases.is_file() {
    40	                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
    41	                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
    42	                }
    43	            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
    44	            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    45	        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    46	            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
    47	        }
    48	    }
    49	    let evidence_options=defaults.clone();
    50	    let fixture_inputs=match defaults.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_|!options.flags.contains_key("residency")){
    51	        Some(path)=>match test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test fixture snapshot: {why}");return 1;}},None=>None,
    52	    };
    53	    if let Some(snapshot)=&fixture_inputs {defaults.flags.insert("fixture-input-label".into(),defaults.flags["fixture"].clone());defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    54	    let consumed=if options.flags.contains_key("residency"){String::new()}else{match security::capture(&sources,fixture_inputs.as_ref().map(|s|s.0.as_path())){Ok(key)=>key,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test input fingerprint: {why}");return 1;}}};
    55	    let options = &defaults;
    56	    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    57	    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    58	    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
    59	        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
    60	            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
    61	            return 1;
    62	        }
    63	        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    64	    }
    65	    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") || !options.flags.contains_key("residency") { None } else {
    66	        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
    67	            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
    68	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    69	        }
    70	    };
    71	    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    72	    let opened = match &_owner {
    73	        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
    74	        None if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&!options.flags.contains_key("residency") => {
    75	            snapback4_core::Store::memory().and_then(|store|snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),fixture_now)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
    76	        },
    77	        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    78	    };
    79	    let mut engine = match opened {
    80	        Ok(engine) => engine,
    81	        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    82	    };
    83	    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    84	    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
    85	        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
    86	            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
    87	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    88	        }
    89	    };
    90	    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
    91	        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    92	    }
    93	    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
    94	        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
    95	        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
    96	        if !verbose {
    97	            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    98	                println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":0,\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}");
    99	            }
   100	            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
   101	                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
   102	                return 1;
   103	            }
   104	            return 0;
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Campaign::default();
   108	    let populated_fixture=bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|table|engine.store().rows(table).is_ok_and(|rows|!rows.is_empty()));
   109	    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&populated_fixture{
   110	        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
   111	            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
   112	            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
   113	        }
   114	    }else{
   115	        snapback4_lang::security::challenges::generated_campaign(&bound,&mut security_campaign,true);
   116	    }
   117	    let security_campaign=snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run_selected_after(&bound,None,security_campaign);
   118	    let mut security_campaign=security_campaign;
   119	    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
   120	    if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {if let Err(why)=security::remember(&project,&evidence_options,&security_campaign,security_http_ok,&consumed){eprintln!("security: campaign record unavailable: {why}; check cannot reuse these refutations");}}
   121	    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
   122	    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);
   123	    for why in &security_campaign.boundary_failures {println!("security boundary failure: {why}");}
   124	    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
   125	    let reachable=security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).count();
   126	    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {reachable} reachable violations; {} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing); finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len()-reachable);
   127	    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability.is_reachable()) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
   128	    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}
   129	    let (mut passed, mut failed_cases) = (0, 0);
   130	    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
   131	        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
   132	            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
   133	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
   134	        }
   135	    }
   136	    let ceilings = engine.ceilings();
   137	    let mut failures = 0;
   138	    println!("test: {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "operation (persona)", "examined", "entries", "probes", "steps", "heap B", "output B");
   139	    println!("      {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "ceilings", ceilings.examined, ceilings.index_entries, ceilings.rule_probes, ceilings.steps, ceilings.heap_bytes, ceilings.output_bytes);
   140	    println!("legend: examined=rows visited; entries=index visits; probes=rule lookups; steps=interpreter work; heap B=charged heap; output B=encoded answer.");
     1	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
     2	use super::{Options, Project};
     3	use std::path::Path;
     4	
     5	/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
     6	/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
     7	pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
     8	    match options.flags.get(flag) {
     9	        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
    10	        Some(file) => {
    11	            let path = Path::new(file);
    12	            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
    13	            if path.is_dir() {
    14	                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
    15	            }
    16	            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
    17	            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
    18	            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
    19	            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
    20	            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
    21	            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
    22	        }
    23	    }
    24	}
    25	
    26	pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    27	    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    28	    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
    29	        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    30	    }
    31	    bound
    32	}
    33	
    34	/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
    35	/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
    36	pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    37	    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    38	    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    39	    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    40	    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
    41	}
    42	
    43	/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
    44	/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
    45	pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
    46	impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
    47	impl Candidate {
    48	    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
    49	        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
    50	        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
    51	        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
    52	        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    53	        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    54	        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
    55	        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
    56	        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
    57	        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
    58	        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    59	        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
    60	        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
    61	        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
    62	        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
    63	            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
    64	            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
    65	        }
    66	        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    67	        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
    68	            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    69	            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
    70	        }
    71	        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
    72	        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
    73	        Ok(candidate)
    74	    }
    75	}
    76	fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    77	    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    78	    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
    79	        let entry = entry?;
    80	        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
    81	        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
    82	        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
    83	        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
    84	        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    85	    }
    86	    Ok(())
    87	}

    89	
    90	#[cfg(test)]
    91	mod tests {
    92	    use super::*;
    93	    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    94	        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
    95	        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    96	    }
    97	    #[test]
    98	    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
    99	        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
   100	        let file = root.join("v1.q");
   101	        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();

   161	
   162	fn rebase_native(text: &str, original: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
   163	    let mut manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))?;
   164	    fn inherited(value: &toml::Value) -> bool {
   165	        let marker = |field: &toml::Value| field.get("workspace").and_then(toml::Value::as_bool) == Some(true);
   166	        if value.get("package").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.iter().any(|(key, field)| key != "metadata" && marker(field))) { return true; }
   167	        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies"] {
   168	            if value.get(key).and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.values().any(marker)) { return true; }
   169	        }
   170	        value.get("target").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|targets| targets.values().any(inherited))
   171	    }
   172	    if !workspace && inherited(&manifest) { return Err("workspace-inherited native packages require a complete standalone [workspace] under snapback/native; copy its workspace package/dependency declarations before saved-source deployment".into()); }
   173	    fn dependency_group(group: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
   174	        if let Some(dependencies) = group.as_table_mut() {
   175	            for (_, dependency) in dependencies.iter_mut() {
   176	                if let Some(path) = dependency.as_table_mut().and_then(|fields| fields.get_mut("path")) {
   177	                    let relative = path.as_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be a string")?;
   178	                    let absolute = root.join(relative).canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native dependency {relative}: {e}"))?;
   179	                    let absolute = absolute.strip_prefix(source_root).map(|relative| target_root.join(relative)).unwrap_or(absolute.clone());
   180	                    *path = toml::Value::String(absolute.to_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be UTF-8")?.to_owned());
   181	                }
   182	            }
   183	        }
   184	        Ok(())
   185	    }
   186	    fn groups(table: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
   187	        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies", "replace"] {
   188	            if let Some(group) = table.get_mut(key) { dependency_group(group, root, source_root, target_root)?; }
   189	        }
   190	        Ok(())
   191	    }
   192	    groups(&mut manifest, original, source_root, target_root)?;
   193	    for category in ["target", "patch"] {
   194	        if let Some(table) = manifest.get_mut(category).and_then(toml::Value::as_table_mut) {
   195	            for (_, item) in table.iter_mut() {
   196	                if category == "target" { groups(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
   197	                else { dependency_group(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
   198	            }
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	    if let Some(workspace) = manifest.get_mut("workspace") { groups(workspace, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
   202	    toml::to_string(&manifest).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))
   203	}
   204	
   205	fn rebase_tree(target: &Path, original: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
   206	    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(target.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   207	    let manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("native manifest: {e}"))?;
   208	    let workspace = manifest.get("workspace").is_some();
   209	    fn walk(directory: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
   210	        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
   211	            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   212	            if entry.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() { walk(&entry.path(), source_root, target_root, workspace)?; }
   213	            else if entry.file_name() == "Cargo.toml" {
   214	                let relative = entry.path().strip_prefix(target_root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.to_owned();
   215	                let source = source_root.join(relative).parent().unwrap().to_owned();
   216	                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   217	                let rebased = rebase_native(&text, &source, source_root, target_root, workspace)?;
   218	                std::fs::write(entry.path(), rebased).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   219	            }
   220	        }
   221	        Ok(())
   222	    }
   223	    let original = original.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   224	    walk(target, &original, target, workspace)
   225	}

codex
The CLI record code binds captured source and fixture bytes to the executable and reloads only failure-eligible witnesses. Generated initialization also separates insert-admitted bootstrap rows from payloads made by authored creators. I’m checking the alias/guard handling, history attribution, and ephemeral fork isolation next.

+    original.ephemeral_hub().set_publisher(move |_|{count.fetch_add(1,Ordering::SeqCst);}).unwrap();

+    let before=published.load(Ordering::SeqCst);let durable=counts(&original);

+    assert_eq!(page(&mut fork,"bob",1001).next_expiry,Some(5000));
+    assert_eq!(fork.mutate_ephemeral("setTyping",ctx("bob",1001,&args("r"))).err().unwrap().code(),"E_EPHEMERAL_FULL");

     1	//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.

     3	impl Engine {
     4	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
     5	    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
     6	        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
     7	        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
     8	        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
     9	        Ok(Self {
    10	            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
    11	            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()){self.ephemeral.fork_fixture(&identity,self.generation)?}else{ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)}),
    12	            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
    13	            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
    14	            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
    15	            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
    16	            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
    17	        })
    18	    }
    19	}

    21	impl Engine {
    22	    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    23	    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
    24	        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
    25	        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
    26	        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
    27	        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
    28	        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
    29	            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
    30	            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
    31	        })
    32	    }

//! One process's short-lived rows and commit epoch, shared by every reader.
// @ref LLP 3000.017#4.3 — no SQLite writer, log, replay record or durable receipt.
mod layered;
#[cfg(test)]
mod wake_tests;
use super::*;
use crate::rows::{Bounds, Dir, MemoryRows, Rows};
pub use crate::schema::ephemeral::Version;
use crate::schema::{Schema, Table};
pub use layered::Layered;
use std::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet, VecDeque},
    sync::{Arc, Mutex, MutexGuard},
};

pub const RING_SIZE: usize = 256;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct Change {
    pub preserved: Vec<String>,
    pub epoch: u64,
    pub incarnation: String,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub preserved: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub version: Version,
    pub ring: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
impl Snapshot {
    pub fn preserved_since(&self, epoch: u64, incarnation: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        if incarnation != self.version.incarnation || epoch >= self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        let mut result: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
        let mut previous = epoch;
        for (at, tables) in self.preserved.iter().filter(|(at,_)| *at > epoch) {
            if *at != previous + 1 { return vec![]; }
            if let Some(result) = &mut result { result.retain(|t| tables.contains(t)); } else { result = Some(tables.clone()); }
            previous = *at;
        }
        if previous != self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        result.unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn changed(
        &self,
        epoch: u64,
        incarnation: Option<&str>,
        interest: Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,
    ) -> Vec<String> {
        let allowed = |name: &String| interest.is_none_or(|set| set.contains(name));
        if incarnation.is_some_and(|id| id != self.version.incarnation)
            || epoch > self.version.epoch
            || self
                .ring
                .front()
                .is_some_and(|(first, _)| epoch.saturating_add(1) < *first)
        {
            return match interest {
                Some(tables) => tables.iter().cloned().collect(),
                None => self.tables.clone(),
            };
        }
        self.ring
            .iter()
            .filter(|(at, _)| *at > epoch)
            .flat_map(|(_, tables)| tables.iter())
            .filter(|t| allowed(t))
            .cloned()
            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
            .into_iter()
            .collect()
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Limits {
    pub principal_rows: u64,
    pub principal_bytes: u64,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
}
impl Default for Limits {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            principal_rows: 1000,
            principal_bytes: 4 * 1024 * 1024,
            rows: 100_000,
            bytes: 512 * 1024 * 1024,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
struct Count {
    rows: u64,
    bytes: u64,
}
impl Count {
    fn add(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows += 1;
        self.bytes = self.bytes.saturating_add(bytes);
    }
    fn remove(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows -= 1;
        self.bytes -= bytes;
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
struct Accounting {
    owners: BTreeMap<(String, String), (String, u64)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
struct Plan {
    changes: Vec<((String, String), Option<(String, u64)>)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
impl Accounting {
    fn remove(&mut self, key: &(String, String)) {
        if let Some((principal, bytes)) = self.owners.remove(key) {
            self.total.remove(bytes);
            let count = self.principals.get_mut(&principal).unwrap();
            count.remove(bytes);
            if count.rows == 0 {
                self.principals.remove(&principal);
            }
        }
    }
    fn plan(
        &self,
        layer: &Layered<'_>,
        schema: &Schema,
        viewer: &str,
        limits: Limits,
    ) -> Result<Plan, RuntimeError> {
        let mut plan = Plan {
            changes: Vec::new(),
            principals: BTreeMap::new(),
            total: self.total,
        };
        for key in layer.changed() {
            if let Some((owner, bytes)) = self.owners.get(&key) {
                plan.total.remove(*bytes);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(owner.clone())
                    .or_insert(self.principals[owner])
                    .remove(*bytes);
            }
            let owner = if layer.eph.get(&key.0, &key.1)?.is_some() {
                let size = declared_size(&schema.tables[&key.0]);
                plan.total.add(size);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(viewer.into())
                    .or_insert_with(|| self.principals.get(viewer).copied().unwrap_or_default())
                    .add(size);
                Some((viewer.into(), size))
            } else {
                None
            };
            plan.changes.push((key, owner));
        }
        if plan.total.rows > limits.rows
            || plan.total.bytes > limits.bytes
            || plan
                .principals
                .values()
                .any(|c| c.rows > limits.principal_rows || c.bytes > limits.principal_bytes)
        {
            return Err(full());
        }
        Ok(plan)
    }
    fn apply(&mut self, plan: Plan) {
        for (key, owner) in plan.changes {
            match owner {
                Some(owner) => {
                    self.owners.insert(key, owner);
                }
            accounting: Accounting::default(),
            version: Version {
                epoch: 0,
                incarnation: incarnation(),
            },
            ring: VecDeque::new(),
            preserved: VecDeque::new(),
            publisher: None,
        }
    }
    pub fn version(&self) -> Version {
        self.version.clone()
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Snapshot {
        Snapshot {
            identity: self.identity.clone(),
            generation: self.generation,
            version: self.version.clone(),
            ring: self.ring.clone(),
            preserved: self.preserved.clone(),
            tables: self.tables.keys().cloned().collect(),
        }
    }
    fn publish(&self) {
        if let Some(publish) = &self.publisher { publish(self.snapshot()); }
    }
    fn rebind(&mut self, identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) {
        let publisher = self.publisher.take();
        *self = Self::new(identity, generation, schema);
        self.publisher = publisher;
        self.publish();
    }
    // Snapshot identity has already been established by the deferred transaction.
    pub fn bind(
        &mut self,
        identity: &str,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if identity != self.identity || generation < self.generation {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "ephemeral hub serves a newer store or generation; retry with a fresh reader"
                    .into(),
            ));
        }
        if generation > self.generation {
            self.rebind(identity.into(), generation, schema);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn sweep(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
        let mut removed = 0;
        for (name, table) in &self.tables {
            loop {
                let rows = self.rows.scan(
                    name,
                    "$expiry",
                    &[],
                    &Bounds {
                        lte: Some(Value::Int(now)),
                        ..Bounds::default()
                    },
                    Dir::Asc,
                    256,
                )?;
                if rows.is_empty() {
                    break;
                }
                for (row, _) in rows {
                    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
                    self.rows.delete(name, table, &row)?;
                    self.accounting.remove(&(name.clone(), id));
                    removed += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(removed)
    }
}
impl Hub {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — campaign copies preserve deadlines, quotas and epochs without retaining the live publisher.
    pub(super) fn fork_fixture(&self,identity:&str,generation:u64)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let state=self.lock()?;
        if state.identity!=identity||state.generation!=generation{return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs a hub bound to the selected store generation".into()));}
        Ok(Self{limits:self.limits,state:Mutex::new(HubState{rows:state.rows.fork_fixture(),identity:state.identity.clone(),generation:state.generation,tables:state.tables.clone(),accounting:state.accounting.clone(),version:state.version.clone(),ring:state.ring.clone(),preserved:state.preserved.clone(),publisher:None})})
    }
    pub fn new(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) -> Self {
        Self::with_limits(identity, generation, schema, Limits::default())
    }
    pub fn with_limits(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema, limits: Limits) -> Self {
        Self {
            state: Mutex::new(HubState::new(identity, generation, schema)),
            limits,
        }
    }
    pub fn lock(&self) -> Result<MutexGuard<'_, HubState>, RuntimeError> {
        self.state
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("ephemeral hub lock poisoned".into()))
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<Snapshot, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(self.lock()?.snapshot())
    }
    /// Install before serving. Called under the hub lock after each commit or
    /// rebind; the callback may take the wake mutex, never the hub or engine.
    // @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — publish before releasing the hub.
    pub fn set_publisher(&self, publisher: impl Fn(Snapshot) + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let mut hub = self.lock()?;
        hub.publisher = Some(Arc::new(publisher));
        hub.publish();
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn clear_and_rebind(
        &self,
        identity: String,
        generation: u64,
     1	//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
     3	use super::*;
     4	const LIMIT:usize=512;
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
     6	pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}
     7	
     8	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
     9	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    10	    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    11	    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
    12	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
    13	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
    14	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    15	    }}
    16	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    17	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    18	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    19	    out.into_iter().collect()
    20	}
    21	pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    22	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    23	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
    24	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    25	    }}}
    26	    out
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    let args=&p.args;
    30	    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    31	    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    32	        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
    33	            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
    34	                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
    35	            }
    36	        }}
    37	        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    38	    }
    39	    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    40	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    41	        Some(match k{
    42	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    43	            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
    44	            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
    45	            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
    46	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    47	            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
    48	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    49	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    50	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    51	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    52	        })
    53	    }
    54	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    55	    for (name,k) in args{
    56	        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    57	        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    58	            let valid=match (k,literal){
    59	                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
    60	                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
    61	                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
    62	            };
    63	            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
    64	        }
    65	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    66	        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
    67	            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
    68	            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
    69	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
    70	        }}
    71	        cases=next;
    72	    }cases
    73	}
    74	pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
    75	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    76	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    77	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
    78	}
    79	fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    80	    use crate::types::Ty;
    81	    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    82	    match ty{
    83	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
    84	        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
    85	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
    86	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
    87	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
    88	        _=>false,
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    92	    use crate::types::Ty;
    93	    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
    94	}
    95	pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    96	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    97	    loop{let before=out.len();
    98	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
    99	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
   100	        if out.len()==before{return out;}
   101	    }
   102	}
   103	fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
   104	    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
   105	    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
   106	}
   107	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   108	    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
   109	    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
   110	        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
   111	        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
   112	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
   113	        }else{true}
   114	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   115	    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
   116	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
   117	        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
   118	        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
   119	        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
   120	
   121	        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
   122	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
   123	            if out.exhausted(){return;}
   124	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
   125	            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
   126	            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
   127	            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
   128	            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}
   129	
   130	            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
   131	            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
   132	            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
   133	                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
   134	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
   135	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   136	                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
   137	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
   138	                    out.attempted+=1;
   139	                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
   140	                        out.attempted+=1;
   141	                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
   142	                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
   143	                        }
   144	                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
   145	                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
   146	                        continue 'actors;
   147	                    }
   148	                }
   149	            }
   150	            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
   151	            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
   152	            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
   153	            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
   154	            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   155	            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
   156	            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
   157	            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   158	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   159	            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
   160	                let p=&bp.program;
   161	                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
   162	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
   163	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   164	                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
   165	                    let raw_args=args;
   166	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
   167	                    if args!=raw_args{
   168	                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
   169	                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
   170	                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
   171	                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
   172	                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
   173	                    }
   174	                    out.attempted+=2;
   175	                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
   176	                    if a==b{continue;}
   177	                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
   178	                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
   179	                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
   180	                    continue 'actors;
   181	                }
   182	            }
   183	        }}
   184	    }
   185	}
   186	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   187	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   188	}
   189	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   190	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   191	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   192	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   193	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   194	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   195	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   196	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   197	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   198	            }
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	}
   202	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   203	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   204	    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
   205	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
   206	        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
   207	        let judged;
   208	        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
   209	            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
   210	            judged=stable;&judged
   211	        };
   212	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
   213	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   214	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
   215	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
   216	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
   217	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   218	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   219	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   220	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   221	            }
   222	        }
   223	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   224	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
   225	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
   226	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
   227	            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
   228	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
   229	            if inv.target=="always" {
   230	                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
   231	            }else{
   232	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   233	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
   234	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
   235	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
   236	                }
   237	            }
   238	        }
   239	    }
   240	}

   106	fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){

   210	fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
   211	    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
   212	    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
   213	}
   214	pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
   215	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
   216	        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
   217	        for variant in 0..16{
   218	            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
   219	            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   220	            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   221	            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   222	            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
   223	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
   224	        }
   225	    }

   227	pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   228	    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   229	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
   230	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
   231	    }
   232	}

   381	    }
   382	    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
   383	    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
   384	    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
   385	    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
   386	    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
   387	    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
   388	    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
   389	    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
   390	        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   391	        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
   392	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   393	            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
   394	            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
   395	            let mut k=0;
   396	            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
   397	        }
   398	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
   399	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   400	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   401	        for i in indices{
   402	            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   403	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   404	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   405	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   406	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   407	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   408	                Ok(result)=>{
   409	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   410	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   411	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   412	                    if sent&&n<singles{
   413	                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
   414	                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
   415	                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   416	                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
   417	                        });
   418	                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
   419	                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
   420	                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   421	                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
   422	                        });
   423	                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
   424	                    }
   425	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   426	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   427	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   428	            }
   429	        }
   430	    }
   431	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   432	}

     1	//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
     6	pub struct Observations {
     7	    pub present: serde_json::Value,
     8	    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
     9	    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
